For each code, first use the selected Finding URL for that item's analytic when such a Finding exists. Otherwise `COLD_START_FAILURE` and `COLD_TRANSFER_UNKNOWN` use the selected backup-chain read URL; `COLD_RECOMPUTE_PENDING`, `COLD_PROBE_GAP` and `COLD_POLICY_MISSING` use the selected Evidence read URL. ### Reading verdicts (Run 5)

1. **CORRECT capability details; ACCEPT the state scale.** `TEMPERATURE_POINT` needs at least one *qualifying* accepted calibrated probe point with attested clock, not merely any known point. `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` needs selected policy, at least two qualifying points, ratio `>=0.8` and no bridge `>10 minutes`. `BACKUP_START_RESULT` needs a correlated observed success/failure chain; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own observed transfer step; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs accepted compressor state and at least two qualifying points; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` needs an eligible OBSERVED/BOUNDED excursion with an exposure Finding **and proved thermal bounds**, not stock loss. Partial evidence is LIMITED only where it supports a narrower claim; absent prerequisites are UNAVAILABLE; contradictory selected W2-03 facts/steps are CONTRADICTORY. `required_roles` use `REQUIRED_ROLES` (the two policy keys are declared policy prerequisites, not source channels); event claim coverage for BACKUP_START_RESULT and TRANSFER_POSITION is `{known_points:null,expected_points:null,ratio:null}`. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 1 and Analysis panel 2. A missing clock/calibration must not produce SUPPORTED from a numeric point count.
2. **ACCEPT backup chain selection** with deterministic tie handling: choose the selected chain with greatest `(anchor_time,correlation_id)`; `latest_chain_id` is its correlation ID and `causal_strip` contains only that chain's ordered steps. If no chain, ID is null and strip is empty. This fills `cold` panel 1.
3. **ACCEPT last accepted fact time.** `/evidence.sources[].last_accepted_at` is the maximum accepted `source_time` for that `(source_id,role)` within the half-open window and cutoff, never `decided_at`; `fact_age` is `max(0,to-last_accepted_at)` whole seconds or null. Label it `Last accepted fact`, separate from `Contact checked at window end {to}`. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 2.
4. **ACCEPT source row identity/order.** Return selected runs' bound `(source_id,role)` channels, deduplicated and sorted by `cold-roles-1` order, then role and source UUID. The mock's six grouped display rows remain in their order; the inverter/battery display row may contain multiple distinct channel details, with no fabricated combined observed device. A configured but unbound mock slot says `No source-specific record`; `Not connected` requires configuration proof. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 2.
5. **CORRECT five UNKNOWN-contact sentences** to exact plain copy: `CONTACT_NO_DIAGNOSTIC` → `No gateway diagnostic was available by the window end and evidence cutoff.`; `CONTACT_NOT_REPORTED` → `The selected gateway diagnostic has no contact time for this source.`; `CONTACT_NO_CADENCE` → `The gateway's reporting interval is not declared, so source contact cannot be called recent.`; `CONTACT_CLOCK` → `The gateway time cannot be verified, so the reported contact time is uncertain.`; `CONTACT_AFTER_WINDOW` → `The reported source contact happened after this window ended.` Use exactly one as `sources[].limitation` with `contact:'UNKNOWN',contact_at:null`. Explicit source entry `null` is `NEVER`, `contact_at:null`, with text `No source contact recorded`; no diagnostic/read failure is not NEVER. `RECENT` includes equality at `2 × declared cadence`; `STALE` is later. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 2 Status and its detail.
6. **ACCEPT pending money; CORRECT group selection.** Current money is WITHHELD if assessment currentness is PENDING_RECOMPUTE or overlapping consequence work has no SUCCEEDED attempt; reason `New evidence is waiting to be assessed`, or the existing `SCREEN_WORDING['COLD_RECOMPUTE_FAILED']` after a failed latest attempt. Within each overlap group, take one PRODUCT_EXPOSURE head from the exact selected Finding revisions, ordered by `(created_at,id)` descending after the highest `revision_no` per Finding/kind; do not add a backup context row to the exposure count. If multiple equally eligible exposure heads represent different Findings in one group, selecting the latest is display deduplication only; retain their separate exact bases in storage. This fills `cold` panel 4 and `cold-financials` panel 1.
7. **ACCEPT cutoff handling with a condition.** `cold_heads_on` is keyed by the exact FindingRevision selected at the cutoff; do not filter its consequence by `created_at<=known_at`, because derivation may finish later. An uncompleted or failed consequence job still withholds money. Analysis record links stay UNAVAILABLE until Q-R9 proof; existing null link and exact limitation are correct. This fills the historical money and Analysis states.
8. **ACCEPT watermark rule.** Reject `known_at>watermark` with 422 `COLD_TIME_INVALID` only when a watermark exists; a valid empty selection with no watermark returns 200 UNKNOWN. Reject submicrosecond precision independently. The selected `known_at` remains visible in the date bar, including pending/historical reads, on all five routes.

# W2-04 phase handoff - run 7 (spec commits 1-4 COMPLETE; commit 5 = final proof + packet NOT STARTED)

## Run 7 (this run): spec commit 4 built (five cold Site screens + retro 14/17)

- Commits `1abdd28b`..`019a9e47` (code), plus the evidence commit. Not merged.
- Code: `frontend/src/sites/coldSiteClient.ts` (five reads, `getAnalysis` takes `cursor`), `sites/coldSiteViews.tsx`
  (`ColdSiteFrame`, `coldUiHref` maps each API link to its UI route keeping `from,to,known_at`/`at`), `sites/w2-cold-site.css`,

### Reading verdicts (Run 5)

1. **CORRECT capability details; ACCEPT the state scale.** `TEMPERATURE_POINT` needs at least one *qualifying* accepted calibrated probe point with attested clock, not merely any known point. `EXCURSION_BOUNDS` needs selected policy, at least two qualifying points, ratio `>=0.8` and no bridge `>10 minutes`. `BACKUP_START_RESULT` needs a correlated observed success/failure chain; `TRANSFER_POSITION` needs its own observed transfer step; `COOLING_RESPONSE` needs accepted compressor state and at least two qualifying points; `PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` needs an eligible OBSERVED/BOUNDED excursion with an exposure Finding **and proved thermal bounds**, not stock loss. Partial evidence is LIMITED only where it supports a narrower claim; absent prerequisites are UNAVAILABLE; contradictory selected W2-03 facts/steps are CONTRADICTORY. `required_roles` use `REQUIRED_ROLES` (the two policy keys are declared policy prerequisites, not source channels); event claim coverage for BACKUP_START_RESULT and TRANSFER_POSITION is `{known_points:null,expected_points:null,ratio:null}`. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 1 and Analysis panel 2. A missing clock/calibration must not produce SUPPORTED from a numeric point count.
2. **ACCEPT backup chain selection** with deterministic tie handling: choose the selected chain with greatest `(anchor_time,correlation_id)`; `latest_chain_id` is its correlation ID and `causal_strip` contains only that chain's ordered steps. If no chain, ID is null and strip is empty. This fills `cold` panel 1.
3. **ACCEPT last accepted fact time.** `/evidence.sources[].last_accepted_at` is the maximum accepted `source_time` for that `(source_id,role)` within the half-open window and cutoff, never `decided_at`; `fact_age` is `max(0,to-last_accepted_at)` whole seconds or null. Label it `Last accepted fact`, separate from `Contact checked at window end {to}`. This fills `cold-evidence` panel 2.
4. **ACCEPT source row identity/order.** Return selected runs' bound `(source_id,role)` channels, deduplicated and sorted by `cold-roles-1` order, then role and source UUID. 5. **CORRECT five UNKNOWN-contact sentences** to exact plain copy: `CONTACT_NO_DIAGNOSTIC` → `No gateway diagnostic was available by the window end and evidence cutoff.`; `CONTACT_NOT_REPORTED` → `The selected gateway diagnostic has no contact time for this source.`; `CONTACT_NO_CADENCE` → `The gateway's reporting interval is not declared, so source contact cannot be called recent.`; `CONTACT_CLOCK` → `The gateway time cannot be verified, so the reported contact time is uncertain.`; `CONTACT_AFTER_WINDOW` → `The reported source contact happened after this window ended.` Use exactly one as `sources[].limitation` with `contact:'UNKNOWN',contact_at:null`. This fills `cold` panel 4 and `cold-financials` panel 1.

Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

| Role / asset type | Kind, unit, cardinality | Need |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `chamber_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required, one calibrated probe per `COLD_ROOM` via declared probe-to-room link |
| `cold_policy_low`,`cold_policy_high` / `COLD_ROOM` | Foundation control number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required; low < high, same effective revision |
| `compressor_state` / `COMPRESSOR` | point state `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, PER_ASSET | required for cooling claim |
| `compressor_power` / `COMPRESSOR` | interval mean `kW`, PER_ASSET | optional corroboration |
| `grid_state` / `METER` | point state `AVAILABLE|LOST`, ONE | required for outage step |
| `backup_start_event` / `GENERATOR` | EVENT `START_REQUEST|START_RESULT`, `event`, ONE per Site | required single channel for start-response claim; both kinds required, `correlation_id` separates attempts |
| `generator_state` / `GENERATOR` | point state `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, ONE | required for observed running state |
| `backup_bus_state` / `AC_BUS` | point state `POWERED|UNPOWERED`, ONE | required for supply-to-cooling claim |
| `grid_power_event` / `METER` | EVENT `POWER_LOSS|POWER_RESTORED`, `event`, ONE | required for witnessed outage and restoration; separate from `grid_state` |
| `transfer_result` / `TRANSFER_SWITCH` | EVENT `TRANSFER_RESULT`, ONE per Site | optional: absent blocks switch-position claim; `correlation_id` separates attempts |
| `door_event` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT open/close, PER_ASSET | optional context |
| `ambient_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context, never substitutes chamber probe |
| `manual_temperature` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT manual log `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context; never silently closes a probe gap |

Foundation topology uses explicit `COLD_ROOM`â†’`TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`â†’room, busâ†’compressor, generatorâ†’transferâ†’bus relations; every referenced asset must exist, one chamber probe cannot be primary for two rooms, and cycles/duplicate edges are refused. Policy band belongs to a room and is effective for the selected window; changing it creates a Foundation revision and binding set selection, never an in-place update. Validate finite exact positive thermal parameters, `0<=hysteresis<high-low`, probe error `>=0`, timeout `>0`, and cadence 5 minutes for the first Lab profile. Publication readiness must say **â€œAdd a chamber temperature probe and bind its Â°C readingâ€**, **â€œSet a lower and upper temperature policy for this roomâ€**, **â€œBind the generator start request and resultâ€**, or **â€œTransfer position is not reported; switch state is unknownâ€** as applicable. +## Run-7 Architect ruling: commit-4 density, source rows and exact copy (2026-10-18)
+
+This section amends the Commit-4 copy ledger and overrides its conflicting strip, source-row and panel-density wording. The v6.9 closed panel is the default state at 1440 and 390; disclosures are keyboard accessible, closed initially, and do not create a seventh source row or a third Analysis/Financials panel. At 1440 each of the four measured outlier panels must be at most **1.30×** its corresponding mock height with disclosures closed. Re-capture both widths after applying the ruling. The only permitted closed-height excess is for *real selected records*: more than the mock's three accepted Analysis records after the user explicitly expands the list, or source names that cannot fit a single line at 390. The six mock source rows, two actual claim rules and up to two bridge rows are not excess reasons by themselves.
+
+| Panel; current measured height | Closed mock-form content | One disclosure per item and exact detail moved from the closed view |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `cold-evidence` panel 2 Data sources, 1423/438 (3.25×) | Exactly six mock rows and five columns. In each cell show at most one primary line: source label; contact chip; quality chip; accepted role summary or `No accepted report for this role`; affected capability names or `Claim effect not assessed`. For multiple distinct channels, summarize only if their states agree; otherwise chip `Mixed source contact` or `Mixed accepted quality`, never a fabricated aggregate `Recent`. One shared line above the table says `Contact checked at window end {to}`. The existing footer remains. | In the Source cell, one `Source details` disclosure **per mock row**, closed. Inside list each `(source_id,role)` separately, with configured asset/device name, contact word, `contact_at` or `No source contact recorded`, its exact Q-R5 limitation when present, accepted count/quality, `Last accepted fact {last_accepted_at} · {fact_age} s old` or `No accepted fact in this window`, role contribution and affected claim. State `Configured source, no accepted report` when bound with no accepted fact. Do not repeat the checked-at line in each channel. |
+| `cold-evidence-analysis` panel 1 Accepted records, 534/266 (2.01×) | Exact six mock headers; first **three** selected accepted records in `(source_time,evidence_id)` order, one line per cell. Keep actual value, Measured (EAT), Sent, Received and exact Record target/gap in their cells. A record ID too long for the cell may be shortened visually but remains readable in its disclosure and accessible name. Below the table, when more rows exist, one `Show all {N} accepted records` control; `N` is the full selected accepted count, not just the current page. | One `Record details` disclosure **per displayed accepted row**, closed, contains `Quality: {quality} · Use: {exclusion or 'Not classified'} · Assessment: {assessment_revision_id}`, exact evidence ID, source/provenance and any Q-R9 limitation. The `Show all` control expands the list without changing the first three or selected basis; if the API has a next cursor, the sole subsequent control reads `Show more accepted records`. Keep the cursor fence and empty/gap behavior already ruled. |
+| `cold-evidence-analysis` panel 2 How a claim limit is set, 693/148 (4.68×) | Keep the mock's two-prose-line form: `Each claim needs accepted readings and enough coverage for the selected window. A missing or conflicting input limits what can be concluded.` and `Source reporting describes data availability, not equipment condition.` If there is no selected assessment, show `Claim limit not assessed` instead of those sentences. The panel does not expand just because two rules exist. | One `Claim details` disclosure **per selected rule**, closed, labelled with `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1` plus its `result`. Inside show Q-R7 exact requirement, observed/missing roles, coverage, result, source classification, evidence cutoff, exact accepted IDs and limitation. These facts remain inspectable and must never become a generic Full claim in the closed prose. |
+| `cold-financials` panel 1 bridge, 381/234 (1.63×) | Exact six mock headers and up to the two ruled rows. Use one primary line in each cell: exposure quantity, undeclared translation, `Not priced`/`Withheld`, `Unpriced`/`Withheld` basis chip, selected claim level, exact Finding link; backup gap uses the already ruled six short cell values. Do not put reason paragraphs or UUID/hash strings in closed cells. Keep the no-row state and real Finding link. | One `Basis details` disclosure **per exposure row**, closed in the Basis cell. Inside show the exact Q-R5 basis fields with labels, selected reason/limitation, quantity bounds and source gate. The backup gap row needs no disclosure because it has no stored consequence or exact cost basis. No value, reason or source limitation is discarded. |
+
+**Evidence status chip.** Compute the four panel-1 row chips first, using the existing pair/solo capability rules. The strip count partitions those **four rows**: `{full} full · {limited} limited · {none} none`, append `{contradictory} contradictory` when nonzero; the counts total four. Strip chip precedence is `Evidence contradictory` if any row is Contradictory; else `Evidence limited` if any row is Limited; else `Evidence unknown` if any row is None or there is no assessment; else `Evidence supported` only when all four rows are Full. Thus the excursion replay's three Full and one Limited rows yield `Evidence limited`, matching Overview; the underlying six typed capabilities stay available in details. The ledger's six-capability count/chip sentence is superseded.
+
+**Exact source-to-mock-row placement.** `Chamber probe` owns only the bound `chamber_temperature` channel in its closed cells. `Generator controller` owns `backup_start_event,generator_state`; `Compressor meter` owns `compressor_state,compressor_power`; `Transfer switch` owns `transfer_result`; `Loading / door log` owns `door_event`. Keep `Inverter-charger + battery` as the mock's second label and state `No inverter or battery source declared` in its closed cells for the W2-04 v4 mapping: no such hardware/channel was declared. Put `grid_state,grid_power_event,backup_bus_state` in **that row's** `Source details` under `Other power-path reports`, each identified as `Grid meter` or `AC bus` from its actual binding; these reports do not populate the inverter/battery row's contact, quality, provides or affects cells and do not establish an inverter/battery observation. Put `ambient_temperature,manual_temperature` in the **Chamber probe row's** `Source details` under `Other temperature context`, identified as `Ambient probe` or `Manual room log` from actual binding; neither populates chamber-probe contact/quality/coverage nor closes a chamber-probe gap. Their separate accepted facts and role effects remain inspectable there. A missing binding says `No source-specific record`; `Not connected` requires proved absent configuration. Do not reclassify grid, bus, ambient or manual reports as the named mock device. The source detail disclosure shows actual source identity and role even when the closed mock row has no declared inverter device.
+
+**Status/date copy.** Keep each route's mock status chips and its one-line status description. Show the selected half-open UTC window in the shared Site date-bar position, labelled `Selected window {from}–{to} UTC`; show `Selected evidence through {known_at} UTC` as the date bar's secondary line or accessible detail on all five routes, including Overview, pending and historical states. The status description remains the ruled claim/status sentence and must not carry the window/cutoff in its place. `cold-assumptions` has the mock's one panel and notice, with the same shared date bar; it gets no new status strip. Preserve the exact selected `from,to,known_at` in links; the global picker may not display a different window as selected. If the shared date-bar component cannot represent the sub-day window/cutoff, add a compact selected-window line immediately below the tabs in that same date-bar slot, not another panel or status strip.
+
+**Overview link and card copy.** A Q-R1 attention item with a selected Finding link says `Open Finding →`; with a selected backup-chain destination says `Performance › Backup →`; with a selected Evidence destination says `Evidence →`. If its exact destination is null, show `Not available yet` as noninteractive text. The Next step heading is the first ordered Q-R1 text with a real destination, with that same destination label; if none, `No recorded next step`. Panel-1 Finding footer uses `Open Finding →` only for an exact Finding. Evidence panel 1/3 uses `Review evidence` for its selected Analysis route; `Review financials` uses the selected Financials route. `Configuration at window end` stays in Evidence navigation, not in a source or claim cell. Temperature card detail is `{episode_count} excursion(s) · latest {latest_deg_c} °C · policy {low}–{high} °C · coverage {known_points} / {expected_points} readings`, omitting each unavailable clause rather than rendering null or zero; when no reading, use the existing `No chamber reading for this window`. Backup card detail is `{attempts} start attempt(s) · {observed_success} started · {observed_failure} failed`, with actual zero counts allowed only from an assessed selected summary; otherwise `Backup result not observed`. These details state counts and readings, never a duration, manual recovery or equipment diagnosis.
+
+**Time copy.** The causal strip and chamber chart both display UTC clock times with visible `UTC` in their labels; their underlying selected instants remain UTC. Only the Analysis table's explicitly labelled `Measured (EAT)` column converts source time to East Africa Time. `Sent` and `Received` show UTC with a visible `UTC` header suffix or per-cell suffix; selection and cutoff stay UTC. No unlabelled mix of EAT and UTC within a panel.
+
 **Copy-source closure for dynamic rows.** Extend `/evidence.sources[]` with `quality:{good_count:int,other_count:int}` from accepted public records for that exact `(source_id,role)` in the selected window/cutoff; `good_count` counts `quality='GOOD'`, `other_count` counts other accepted qualities, and both are zero when none. Panel 2 Quality is `No accepted fact` for total zero, `Accepted facts · good quality` for good-only, `Accepted facts · limited quality` for other-only, or `Accepted facts · mixed quality` for both. The source role is the sole source of What it provides: `chamber_temperature` → `Chamber temperature`; `grid_power_event` → `Grid event`; `backup_start_event` → `Start request and result`; `generator_state` → `Generator state`; `transfer_result` → `Transfer position`; `backup_bus_state` → `Bus voltage`; `compressor_state` → `Compressor state`; `door_event` → `Door and loading context`. For any other bound role, use its exact configured role label prefixed `Configured role: ` and never claim an accepted report. The Affects cell is the comma-separated capability display labels whose `required_roles` contain the row's role, in panel-1 order; if none, `Claim effect not assessed`. Missing-role display uses the same role names. Panel-1 What-we-can-say sentences are fixed by state: for Full `Selected records support this claim for the covered window.`; Limited `Selected records support only part of this claim.`; None `This claim is not established from the selected records.`; Contradictory `Selected records contradict one another.` Append `Thermal exposure does not prove product loss.` on the Product exposure row for every state. Panel-1 What-is-missing uses selected missing-role labels joined with `, `; if empty for Full use `Nothing required for this claim`, and if empty for Limited/None use `Coverage or claim basis incomplete`. The panel-1 Next-step cell is `Review evidence` for the first three rows and `Review financials` for Product exposure, using the selected exact link or noninteractive `Not available yet`. The Overview event-strip prose is fixed as `The steps below are based on the selected accepted records. Missing steps remain unobserved.`; when the strip is empty use `The power-to-cooling sequence is not established for this window.` Thus no implementer-written narrative fills a mock slot.

 All five routes: an empty selected window shows `No cold assessment for this window` in relevant `cold`/`cold-evidence` panels, `No accepted records for this window` and `Claim limit not assessed` on Analysis, `No assumption in use` on Financials and Assumptions, and `No recorded next step` on Overview. Pending shows `New evidence is waiting to be assessed`; failed cold work uses the ruled `COLD_RECOMPUTE_FAILED` sentence; historical shows `Earlier evidence cutoff`; stale shows `Latest usable probe is more than 10 minutes before this window ended`. A 403 source disclosure retains all indexed panels and shows `Source details are not available for this Site` in affected cells without IDs. A 503 retains them and shows `Cold Site data is temporarily unavailable` plus `Retry`. No 503 becomes a successful empty read. The tab/date bar uses selected `from`, `to`, `known_at` everywhere; unsupported tabs read `Not available yet` and are noninteractive. `Loading / batch` in the separate cold Performance context table is the exact mock label, with `Not established` when no loading record; the capture/settling detector matches only a real loading-state element or exact loading sentences, never any text containing `Loading`. Recapture affected screens at 1440 and 390.
 
+## Run-7 Architect ruling: commit-4 density, source rows and exact copy (2026-10-18)
+
+This section amends the Commit-4 copy ledger and overrides its conflicting strip, source-row and panel-density wording. The v6.9 closed panel is the default state at 1440 and 390; each named panel has exactly **one** keyboard-accessible disclosure, closed initially, and no disclosure creates a seventh source row or a third Analysis/Financials panel. At 1440 each of the four measured outlier panels must be at most **1.30×** its corresponding mock height with disclosures closed. Re-capture both widths after applying the ruling. The only permitted excess after expansion is for *real selected records* beyond the mock's three accepted Analysis records; the six mock source rows, two actual claim rules and up to two bridge rows are not excess reasons by themselves. At 390, long real source names may wrap but must remain readable without page overflow.
+
+| Panel; current measured height | Closed mock-form content | The panel's single closed disclosure and exact detail moved from the closed view |
+| --- | --- | --- |
+| `cold-evidence` panel 2 Data sources, 1423/438 (3.25×) | Exactly six mock rows and five columns. In each cell show at most one primary line: source label; contact chip; quality chip; accepted role summary or `No accepted report for this role`; affected capability names or `Claim effect not assessed`. For multiple distinct channels, summarize only if their states agree; otherwise chip `Mixed source contact` or `Mixed accepted quality`, never a fabricated aggregate `Recent`. One shared line above the table says `Contact checked at window end {to}`. The existing footer remains. | One `Source details` disclosure below the table, closed. Inside, group details by the six mock row labels and list each `(source_id,role)` separately with configured asset/device name, contact word, `contact_at` or `No source contact recorded`, exact Q-R5 limitation when present, accepted count/quality, `Last accepted fact {last_accepted_at} · {fact_age} s old` or `No accepted fact in this window`, role contribution and affected claim. State `Configured source, no accepted report` when bound with no accepted fact. Do not repeat the checked-at line in each channel. |
+| `cold-evidence-analysis` panel 1 Accepted records, 534/266 (2.01×) | Exact six mock headers; first **three** selected accepted records in `(source_time,evidence_id)` order, one line per cell. Keep actual value, Measured (EAT), Sent, Received and exact Record target/gap in their cells. A record ID too long for the cell may be shortened visually but remains readable in its disclosure and accessible name. Below the table, when more rows exist, one `Show all {N} accepted records` control; `N` is the full selected accepted count, not just the current page. | One `Record details` disclosure below the table, closed, lists every currently displayed accepted row by exact ID, with `Quality: {quality} · Use: {exclusion or 'Not classified'} · Assessment: {assessment_revision_id}`, source/provenance and any Q-R9 limitation. The `Show all` control expands the list without changing the first three or selected basis; if the API has a next cursor, the sole subsequent control reads `Show more accepted records`. Keep the cursor fence and empty/gap behavior already ruled. |
+| `cold-evidence-analysis` panel 2 How a claim limit is set, 693/148 (4.68×) | Keep the mock's two-prose-line form: `Each claim needs accepted readings and enough coverage for the selected window. A missing or conflicting input limits what can be concluded.` and `Source reporting describes data availability, not equipment condition.` If there is no selected assessment, show `Claim limit not assessed` instead of those sentences. The panel does not expand just because two rules exist. | One `Claim details` disclosure, closed, lists each selected rule by `cold-excursion/1` or `cold-start-response/1` and its `result`. Inside show Q-R7 exact requirement, observed/missing roles, coverage, result, source classification, evidence cutoff, exact accepted IDs and limitation. These facts remain inspectable and must never become a generic Full claim in the closed prose. |
+| `cold-financials` panel 1 bridge, 381/234 (1.63×) | Exact six mock headers and up to the two ruled rows. Use one primary line in each cell: exposure quantity, undeclared translation, `Not priced`/`Withheld`, `Unpriced`/`Withheld` basis chip, selected claim level, exact Finding link; backup gap uses the already ruled six short cell values. Do not put reason paragraphs or UUID/hash strings in closed cells. Keep the no-row state and real Finding link. | One `Basis details` disclosure below the table, closed. Inside show each exposure row's exact Q-R5 basis fields with labels, selected reason/limitation, quantity bounds and source gate. The backup gap row has no stored consequence or exact cost basis. No value, reason or source limitation is discarded. |
+
+**Evidence status chip.** Compute the four panel-1 row chips first, using the existing pair/solo capability rules. The strip count partitions those **four rows**: `{full} full · {limited} limited · {none} none`, append `{contradictory} contradictory` when nonzero; the counts total four. Strip chip precedence is `Evidence contradictory` if any row is Contradictory; else `Evidence limited` if any row is Limited; else `Evidence unknown` if any row is None or there is no assessment; else `Evidence supported` only when all four rows are Full. Thus the excursion replay's three Full and one Limited rows yield `Evidence limited`, matching Overview; the underlying six typed capabilities stay available in details. The ledger's six-capability count/chip sentence is superseded.
+
+**Exact source-to-mock-row placement.** `Chamber probe` owns only the bound `chamber_temperature` channel in its closed cells. `Generator controller` owns `backup_start_event,generator_state`; `Compressor meter` owns `compressor_state,compressor_power`; `Transfer switch` owns `transfer_result`; `Loading / door log` owns `door_event`. Keep `Inverter-charger + battery` as the mock's second label and state `No inverter or battery source declared` in its closed cells for the W2-04 v4 mapping: no such hardware/channel was declared. Put `grid_state,grid_power_event,backup_bus_state` in **that row's** `Source details` under `Other power-path reports`, each identified as `Grid meter` or `AC bus` from its actual binding; these reports do not populate the inverter/battery row's contact, quality, provides or affects cells and do not establish an inverter/battery observation. Put `ambient_temperature,manual_temperature` in the **Chamber probe row's** `Source details` under `Other temperature context`, identified as `Ambient probe` or `Manual room log` from actual binding; neither populates chamber-probe contact/quality/coverage nor closes a chamber-probe gap. Their separate accepted facts and role effects remain inspectable there. A missing binding says `No source-specific record`; `Not connected` requires proved absent configuration. Do not reclassify grid, bus, ambient or manual reports as the named mock device. The source detail disclosure shows actual source identity and role even when the closed mock row has no declared inverter device.
+
+**Status/date copy.** Keep each route's mock status chips and its one-line status description. Show the selected half-open UTC window in the shared Site date-bar position, labelled `Selected window {from}–{to} UTC`; show `Selected evidence through {known_at} UTC` as the date bar's secondary line or accessible detail on all five routes, including Overview, pending and historical states. The status description remains the ruled claim/status sentence and must not carry the window/cutoff in its place. `cold-assumptions` has the mock's one panel and notice, with the same shared date bar; it gets no new status strip. Preserve the exact selected `from,to,known_at` in links; the global picker may not display a different window as selected. If the shared date-bar component cannot represent the sub-day window/cutoff, add a compact selected-window line immediately below the tabs in that same date-bar slot, not another panel or status strip.
+
+**Overview link and card copy.** A Q-R1 attention item with a selected Finding link says `Open Finding →`; with a selected backup-chain destination says `Performance › Backup →`; with a selected Evidence destination says `Evidence →`. If its exact destination is null, show `Not available yet` as noninteractive text. The Next step heading is the first ordered Q-R1 text with a real destination, with that same destination label; if none, `No recorded next step`. Panel-1 Finding footer uses `Open Finding →` only for an exact Finding. Evidence panel 1/3 uses `Review evidence` for its selected Analysis route; `Review financials` uses the selected Financials route. `Configuration at window end` stays in Evidence navigation, not in a source or claim cell. Temperature card detail is `{episode_count} excursion(s) · latest {latest_deg_c} °C · policy {low}–{high} °C · coverage {known_points} / {expected_points} readings`, omitting each unavailable clause rather than rendering null or zero; when no reading, use the existing `No chamber reading for this window`. Backup card detail is `{attempts} start attempt(s) · {observed_success} started · {observed_failure} failed`, with actual zero counts allowed only from an assessed selected summary; otherwise `Backup result not observed`. These details state counts and readings, never a duration, manual recovery or equipment diagnosis.
+
+**Time copy.** The causal strip and chamber chart both display UTC clock times with visible `UTC` in their labels; their underlying selected instants remain UTC. Only the Analysis table's explicitly labelled `Measured (EAT)` column converts source time to East Africa Time. `Sent` and `Received` show UTC with a visible `UTC` header suffix or per-cell suffix; selection and cutoff stay UTC. No unlabelled mix of EAT and UTC within a panel.
+
 **Copy-source closure for dynamic rows.** Extend `/evidence.sources[]` with `quality:{good_count:int,other_count:int}` from accepted public records for that exact `(source_id,role)` in the selected window/cutoff; `good_count` counts `quality='GOOD'`, `other_count` counts other accepted qualities, and both are zero when none. Panel 2 Quality is `No accepted fact` for total zero, `Accepted facts · good quality` for good-only, `Accepted facts · limited quality` for other-only, or `Accepted facts · mixed quality` for both. The source role is the sole source of What it provides: `chamber_temperature` → `Chamber temperature`; `grid_power_event` → `Grid event`; `backup_start_event` → `Start request and result`; `generator_state` → `Generator state`; `transfer_result` → `Transfer position`; `backup_bus_state` → `Bus voltage`; `compressor_state` → `Compressor state`; `door_event` → `Door and loading context`. For any other bound role, use its exact configured role label prefixed `Configured role: ` and never claim an accepted report. The Affects cell is the comma-separated capability display labels whose `required_roles` contain the row's role, in panel-1 order; if none, `Claim effect not assessed`. Missing-role display uses the same role names. Panel-1 What-we-can-say sentences are fixed by state: for Full `Selected records support this claim for the covered window.`; Limited `Selected records support only part of this claim.`; None `This claim is not established from the selected records.`; Contradictory `Selected records contradict one another.` Append `Thermal exposure does not prove product loss.` on the Product exposure row for every state. Panel-1 What-is-missing uses selected missing-role labels joined with `, `; if empty for Full use `Nothing required for this claim`, and if empty for Limited/None use `Coverage or claim basis incomplete`. The panel-1 Next-step cell is `Review evidence` for the first three rows and `Review financials` for Product exposure, using the selected exact link or noninteractive `Not available yet`. The Overview event-strip prose is fixed as `The steps below are based on the selected accepted records. Missing steps remain unobserved.`; when the strip is empty use `The power-to-cooling sequence is not established for this window.` Thus no implementer-written narrative fills a mock slot.

+**Evidence status chip.** Compute the four panel-1 row chips first, using the existing pair/solo capability rules. The strip count partitions those **four rows**: `{full} full · {limited} limited · {none} none`, append `{contradictory} contradictory` when nonzero; the counts total four. Strip chip precedence is `Evidence contradictory` if any row is Contradictory; else `Evidence limited` if any row is Limited; else `Evidence unknown` if any row is None or there is no assessment; else `Evidence supported` only when all four rows are Full. Thus the excursion replay's three Full and one Limited rows yield `Evidence limited`, matching Overview; the underlying six typed capabilities stay available in details. The ledger's six-capability count/chip sentence is superseded.
+
+**Exact source-to-mock-row placement.** `Chamber probe` owns only the bound `chamber_temperature` channel in its closed cells. `Generator controller` owns `backup_start_event,generator_state`; `Compressor meter` owns `compressor_state,compressor_power`; `Transfer switch` owns `transfer_result`; `Loading / door log` owns `door_event`. Keep `Inverter-charger + battery` as the mock's second label and state `No inverter or battery source declared` in its closed cells for the W2-04 v4 mapping: no such hardware/channel was declared. Put `grid_state,grid_power_event,backup_bus_state` in **that row's** `Source details` under `Other power-path reports`, each identified as `Grid meter` or `AC bus` from its actual binding; these reports do not populate the inverter/battery row's contact, quality, provides or affects cells and do not establish an inverter/battery observation. Put `ambient_temperature,manual_temperature` in the **Chamber probe row's** `Source details` under `Other temperature context`, identified as `Ambient probe` or `Manual room log` from actual binding; neither populates chamber-probe contact/quality/coverage nor closes a chamber-probe gap. Their separate accepted facts and role effects remain inspectable there. A missing binding says `No source-specific record`; `Not connected` requires proved absent configuration. Do not reclassify grid, bus, ambient or manual reports as the named mock device. The source detail disclosure shows actual source identity and role even when the closed mock row has no declared inverter device.
+
+**Status/date copy.** Keep each route's mock status chips and its one-line status description. Show the selected half-open UTC window in the shared Site date-bar position, labelled `Selected window {from}–{to} UTC`; show `Selected evidence through {known_at} UTC` as the date bar's secondary line or accessible detail on all five routes, including Overview, pending and historical states. The status description remains the ruled claim/status sentence and must not carry the window/cutoff in its place. `cold-assumptions` has the mock's one panel and notice, with the same shared date bar; it gets no new status strip. Preserve the exact selected `from,to,known_at` in links; the global picker may not display a different window as selected. If the shared date-bar component cannot represent the sub-day window/cutoff, add a compact selected-window line immediately below the tabs in that same date-bar slot, not another panel or status strip.
+
+**Overview link and card copy.** A Q-R1 attention item with a selected Finding link says `Open Finding →`; with a selected backup-chain destination says `Performance › Backup →`; with a selected Evidence destination says `Evidence →`. If its exact destination is null, show `Not available yet` as noninteractive text. The Next step heading is the first ordered Q-R1 text with a real destination, with that same destination label; if none, `No recorded next step`. Panel-1 Finding footer uses `Open Finding →` only for an exact Finding. Evidence panel 1/3 uses `Review evidence` for its selected Analysis route; `Review financials` uses the selected Financials route. `Configuration at window end` stays in Evidence navigation, not in a source or claim cell. Temperature card detail is `{episode_count} excursion(s) · latest {latest_deg_c} °C · policy {low}–{high} °C · coverage {known_points} / {expected_points} readings`, omitting each unavailable clause rather than rendering null or zero; when no reading, use the existing `No chamber reading for this window`. Backup card detail is `{attempts} start attempt(s) · {observed_success} started · {observed_failure} failed`, with actual zero counts allowed only from an assessed selected summary; otherwise `Backup result not observed`. These details state counts and readings, never a duration, manual recovery or equipment diagnosis.
+
+**Time copy.** The causal strip and chamber chart both display UTC clock times with visible `UTC` in their labels; their underlying selected instants remain UTC. Only the Analysis table's explicitly labelled `Measured (EAT)` column converts source time to East Africa Time. `Sent` and `Received` show UTC with a visible `UTC` header suffix or per-cell suffix; selection and cutoff stay UTC. No unlabelled mix of EAT and UTC within a panel.
+
 **Copy-source closure for dynamic rows.** Extend `/evidence.sources[]` with `quality:{good_count:int,other_count:int}` from accepted public records for that exact `(source_id,role)` in the selected window/cutoff; `good_count` counts `quality='GOOD'`, `other_count` counts other accepted qualities, and both are zero when none. Panel 2 Quality is `No accepted fact` for total zero, `Accepted facts · good quality` for good-only, `Accepted facts · limited quality` for other-only, or `Accepted facts · mixed quality` for both. The source role is the sole source of What it provides: `chamber_temperature` → `Chamber temperature`; `grid_power_event` → `Grid event`; `backup_start_event` → `Start request and result`; `generator_state` → `Generator state`; `transfer_result` → `Transfer position`; `backup_bus_state` → `Bus voltage`; `compressor_state` → `Compressor state`; `door_event` → `Door and loading context`. For any other bound role, use its exact configured role label prefixed `Configured role: ` and never claim an accepted report. The Affects cell is the comma-separated capability display labels whose `required_roles` contain the row's role, in panel-1 order; if none, `Claim effect not assessed`. Missing-role display uses the same role names. Panel-1 What-we-can-say sentences are fixed by state: for Full `Selected records support this claim for the covered window.`; Limited `Selected records support only part of this claim.`; None `This claim is not established from the selected records.`; Contradictory `Selected records contradict one another.` Append `Thermal exposure does not prove product loss.` on the Product exposure row for every state. Panel-1 What-is-missing uses selected missing-role labels joined with `, `; if empty for Full use `Nothing required for this claim`, and if empty for Limited/None use `Coverage or claim basis incomplete`. The panel-1 Next-step cell is `Review evidence` for the first three rows and `Review financials` for Product exposure, using the selected exact link or noninteractive `Not available yet`. The Overview event-strip prose is fixed as `The steps below are based on the selected accepted records. Missing steps remain unobserved.`; when the strip is empty use `The power-to-cooling sequence is not established for this window.` Thus no implementer-written narrative fills a mock slot.

+**Exact source-to-mock-row placement.** `Chamber probe` owns only the bound `chamber_temperature` channel in its closed cells. `Generator controller` owns `backup_start_event,generator_state`; `Compressor meter` owns `compressor_state,compressor_power`; `Transfer switch` owns `transfer_result`; `Loading / door log` owns `door_event`. Put `grid_state,grid_power_event,backup_bus_state` in **that row's** `Source details` under `Other power-path reports`, each identified as `Grid meter` or `AC bus` from its actual binding; these reports do not populate the inverter/battery row's contact, quality, provides or affects cells and do not establish an inverter/battery observation. Put `ambient_temperature,manual_temperature` in the **Chamber probe row's** `Source details` under `Other temperature context`, identified as `Ambient probe` or `Manual room log` from actual binding; neither populates chamber-probe contact/quality/coverage nor closes a chamber-probe gap. Their separate accepted facts and role effects remain inspectable there. A missing binding says `No source-specific record`; `Not connected` requires proved absent configuration. Do not reclassify grid, bus, ambient or manual reports as the named mock device. The source detail disclosure shows actual source identity and role even when the closed mock row has no declared inverter device.
+
+**Status/date copy.** Keep each route's mock status chips and its one-line status description. Show the selected half-open UTC window in the shared Site date-bar position, labelled `Selected window {from}–{to} UTC`; show `Selected evidence through {known_at} UTC` as the date bar's secondary line or accessible detail on all five routes, including Overview, pending and historical states. The status description remains the ruled claim/status sentence and must not carry the window/cutoff in its place. `cold-assumptions` has the mock's one panel and notice, with the same shared date bar; it gets no new status strip. Preserve the exact selected `from,to,known_at` in links; the global picker may not display a different window as selected. If the shared date-bar component cannot represent the sub-day window/cutoff, add a compact selected-window line immediately below the tabs in that same date-bar slot, not another panel or status strip.
+
+**Overview link and card copy.** A Q-R1 attention item with a selected Finding link says `Open Finding →`; with a selected backup-chain destination says `Performance › Backup →`; with a selected Evidence destination says `Evidence →`. If its exact destination is null, show `Not available yet` as noninteractive text. The Next step heading is the first ordered Q-R1 text with a real destination, with that same destination label; if none, `No recorded next step`. Panel-1 Finding footer uses `Open Finding →` only for an exact Finding. Evidence panel 1/3 uses `Review evidence` for its selected Analysis route; `Review financials` uses the selected Financials route. `Configuration at window end` stays in Evidence navigation, not in a source or claim cell. Temperature card detail is `{episode_count} excursion(s) · latest {latest_deg_c} °C · policy {low}–{high} °C · coverage {known_points} / {expected_points} readings`, omitting each unavailable clause rather than rendering null or zero; when no reading, use the existing `No chamber reading for this window`. Backup card detail is `{attempts} start attempt(s) · {observed_success} started · {observed_failure} failed`, with actual zero counts allowed only from an assessed selected summary; otherwise `Backup result not observed`. These details state counts and readings, never a duration, manual recovery or equipment diagnosis.
+
+**Time copy.** The causal strip and chamber chart both display UTC clock times with visible `UTC` in their labels; their underlying selected instants remain UTC. Only the Analysis table's explicitly labelled `Measured (EAT)` column converts source time to East Africa Time. `Sent` and `Received` show UTC with a visible `UTC` header suffix or per-cell suffix; selection and cutoff stay UTC. No unlabelled mix of EAT and UTC within a panel.
+
 **Copy-source closure for dynamic rows.** Extend `/evidence.sources[]` with `quality:{good_count:int,other_count:int}` from accepted public records for that exact `(source_id,role)` in the selected window/cutoff; `good_count` counts `quality='GOOD'`, `other_count` counts other accepted qualities, and both are zero when none. Panel 2 Quality is `No accepted fact` for total zero, `Accepted facts · good quality` for good-only, `Accepted facts · limited quality` for other-only, or `Accepted facts · mixed quality` for both. The source role is the sole source of What it provides: `chamber_temperature` → `Chamber temperature`; `grid_power_event` → `Grid event`; `backup_start_event` → `Start request and result`; `generator_state` → `Generator state`; `transfer_result` → `Transfer position`; `backup_bus_state` → `Bus voltage`; `compressor_state` → `Compressor state`; `door_event` → `Door and loading context`. For any other bound role, use its exact configured role label prefixed `Configured role: ` and never claim an accepted report. The Affects cell is the comma-separated capability display labels whose `required_roles` contain the row's role, in panel-1 order; if none, `Claim effect not assessed`. Missing-role display uses the same role names. Panel-1 What-we-can-say sentences are fixed by state: for Full `Selected records support this claim for the covered window.`; Limited `Selected records support only part of this claim.`; None `This claim is not established from the selected records.`; Contradictory `Selected records contradict one another.` Append `Thermal exposure does not prove product loss.` on the Product exposure row for every state. Panel-1 What-is-missing uses selected missing-role labels joined with `, `; if empty for Full use `Nothing required for this claim`, and if empty for Limited/None use `Coverage or claim basis incomplete`. The panel-1 Next-step cell is `Review evidence` for the first three rows and `Review financials` for Product exposure, using the selected exact link or noninteractive `Not available yet`. The Overview event-strip prose is fixed as `The steps below are based on the selected accepted records. Missing steps remain unobserved.`; when the strip is empty use `The power-to-cooling sequence is not established for this window.` Thus no implementer-written narrative fills a mock slot.

+**Evidence status chip.** Compute the four panel-1 row chips first, using the existing pair/solo capability rules. The strip count partitions those **four rows**: `{full} full · {limited} limited · {none} none`, append `{contradictory} contradictory` when nonzero; the counts total four. Strip chip precedence is `Evidence contradictory` if any row is Contradictory; else `Evidence limited` if any row is Limited; else `Evidence unknown` if any row is None or there is no assessment; else `Evidence supported` only when all four rows are Full. Thus the excursion replay's three Full and one Limited rows yield `Evidence limited`, matching Overview; the underlying six typed capabilities stay available in details. The ledger's six-capability count/chip sentence is superseded.

Backup card detail is `{attempts} start attempt(s) · {observed_success} started · {observed_failure} failed`, with actual zero counts allowed only from an assessed selected summary; otherwise `Backup result not observed`. These details state counts and readings, never a duration, manual recovery or equipment diagnosis.

No unlabelled mix of EAT and UTC within a panel.

Panel 2 Quality is `No accepted fact` for total zero, `Accepted facts · good quality` for good-only, `Accepted facts · limited quality` for other-only, or `Accepted facts · mixed quality` for both. The source role is the sole source of What it provides: `chamber_temperature` → `Chamber temperature`; `grid_power_event` → `Grid event`; `backup_start_event` → `Start request and result`; `generator_state` → `Generator state`; `transfer_result` → `Transfer position`; `backup_bus_state` → `Bus voltage`; `compressor_state` → `Compressor state`; `door_event` → `Door and loading context`. For any other bound role, use its exact configured role label prefixed `Configured role: ` and never claim an accepted report. The Affects cell is the comma-separated capability display labels whose `required_roles` contain the row's role, in panel-1 order; if none, `Claim effect not assessed`. Missing-role display uses the same role names. Panel-1 What-we-can-say sentences are fixed by state: for Full `Selected records support this claim for the covered window.`; Limited `Selected records support only part of this claim.`; None `This claim is not established from the selected records.`; Contradictory `Selected records contradict one another.` Append `Thermal exposure does not prove product loss.` on the Product exposure row for every state. Panel-1 What-is-missing uses selected missing-role labels joined with `, `; if empty for Full use `Nothing required for this claim`, and if empty for Limited/None use `Coverage or claim basis incomplete`. The panel-1 Next-step cell is `Review evidence` for the first three rows and `Review financials` for Product exposure, using the selected exact link or noninteractive `Not available yet`. The Overview event-strip prose is fixed as `The steps below are based on the selected accepted records. Missing steps remain unobserved.`; when the strip is empty use `The power-to-cooling sequence is not established for this window.` Thus no implementer-written narrative fills a mock slot.

The strip count partitions those **four rows**: `{full} full · {limited} limited · {none} none`, append `{contradictory} contradictory` when nonzero; the counts total four. Strip chip precedence is `Evidence contradictory` if any row is Contradictory; else `Evidence limited` if any row is Limited; else `Evidence unknown` if any row is None or there is no assessment; else `Evidence supported` only when all four rows are Full. Thus the excursion replay's three Full and one Limited rows yield `Evidence limited`, matching Overview; the underlying six typed capabilities stay available in details. The ledger's six-capability count/chip sentence is superseded.
+
+**Exact source-to-mock-row placement.** `Chamber probe` owns only the bound `chamber_temperature` channel in its closed cells. `Generator controller` owns `backup_start_event,generator_state`; `Compressor meter` owns `compressor_state,compressor_power`; `Transfer switch` owns `transfer_result`; `Loading / door log` owns `door_event`. Keep `Inverter-charger + battery` as the mock's second label and state `No inverter or battery source declared` in its closed cells for the W2-04 v4 mapping: no such hardware/channel was declared. Put `grid_state,grid_power_event,backup_bus_state` in **that row's** `Source details` under `Other power-path reports`, each identified as `Grid meter` or `AC bus` from its actual binding; these reports do not populate the inverter/battery row's contact, quality, provides or affects cells and do not establish an inverter/battery observation. Put `ambient_temperature,manual_temperature` in the **Chamber probe row's** `Source details` under `Other temperature context`, identified as `Ambient probe` or `Manual room log` from actual binding; neither populates chamber-probe contact/quality/coverage nor closes a chamber-probe gap. Their separate accepted facts and role effects remain inspectable there. A missing binding says `No source-specific record`; `Not connected` requires proved absent configuration. Do not reclassify grid, bus, ambient or manual reports as the named mock device. The source detail disclosure shows actual source identity and role even when the closed mock row has no declared inverter device.
+
+**Status/date copy.** Keep each route's mock status chips and its one-line status description. Show the selected half-open UTC window in the shared Site date-bar position, labelled `Selected window {from}–{to} UTC`; show `Selected evidence through {known_at} UTC` as the date bar's secondary line or accessible detail on all five routes, including Overview, pending and historical states. The status description remains the ruled claim/status sentence and must not carry the window/cutoff in its place. `cold-assumptions` has the mock's one panel and notice, with the same shared date bar; it gets no new status strip. +**Overview link and card copy.** A Q-R1 attention item with a selected Finding link says `Open Finding →`; with a selected backup-chain destination says `Performance › Backup →`; with a selected Evidence destination says `Evidence →`. If its exact destination is null, show `Not available yet` as noninteractive text. The Next step heading is the first ordered Q-R1 text with a real destination, with that same destination label; if none, `No recorded next step`. Panel-1 Finding footer uses `Open Finding →` only for an exact Finding. Evidence panel 1/3 uses `Review evidence` for its selected Analysis route; `Review financials` uses the selected Financials route. `Configuration at window end` stays in Evidence navigation, not in a source or claim cell. Temperature card detail is `{episode_count} excursion(s) · latest {latest_deg_c} °C · policy {low}–{high} °C · coverage {known_points} / {expected_points} readings`, omitting each unavailable clause rather than rendering null or zero; when no reading, use the existing `No chamber reading for this window`. Backup card detail is `{attempts} start attempt(s) · {observed_success} started · {observed_failure} failed`, with actual zero counts allowed only from an assessed selected summary; otherwise `Backup result not observed`. These details state counts and readings, never a duration, manual recovery or equipment diagnosis.
+
+**Time copy.** The causal strip and chamber chart both display UTC clock times with visible `UTC` in their labels; their underlying selected instants remain UTC. Only the Analysis table's explicitly labelled `Measured (EAT)` column converts source time to East Africa Time. `Sent` and `Received` show UTC with a visible `UTC` header suffix or per-cell suffix; selection and cutoff stay UTC. No unlabelled mix of EAT and UTC within a panel.

Panel 2 Quality is `No accepted fact` for total zero, `Accepted facts · good quality` for good-only, `Accepted facts · limited quality` for other-only, or `Accepted facts · mixed quality` for both. The source role is the sole source of What it provides: `chamber_temperature` → `Chamber temperature`; `grid_power_event` → `Grid event`; `backup_start_event` → `Start request and result`; `generator_state` → `Generator state`; `transfer_result` → `Transfer position`; `backup_bus_state` → `Bus voltage`; `compressor_state` → `Compressor state`; `door_event` → `Door and loading context`. For any other bound role, use its exact configured role label prefixed `Configured role: ` and never claim an accepted report. The Affects cell is the comma-separated capability display labels whose `required_roles` contain the row's role, in panel-1 order; if none, `Claim effect not assessed`. Missing-role display uses the same role names. Panel-1 What-we-can-say sentences are fixed by state: for Full `Selected records support this claim for the covered window.`; Limited `Selected records support only part of this claim.`; None `This claim is not established from the selected records.`; Contradictory `Selected records contradict one another.` Append `Thermal exposure does not prove product loss.` on the Product exposure row for every state. Panel-1 What-is-missing uses selected missing-role labels joined with `, `; if empty for Full use `Nothing required for this claim`, and if empty for Limited/None use `Coverage or claim basis incomplete`. The panel-1 Next-step cell is `Review evidence` for the first three rows and `Review financials` for Product exposure, using the selected exact link or noninteractive `Not available yet`. The Overview event-strip prose is fixed as `The steps below are based on the selected accepted records. Missing steps remain unobserved.`; when the strip is empty use `The power-to-cooling sequence is not established for this window.` Thus no implementer-written narrative fills a mock slot.
